Conclusion: Goedel-Architect 通过蓝图生成与精化策略显著提升了形式化定理证明的性能与效率，在多个高难度基准上达到了开源 SOTA，同时大幅降低了计算成本，证明了蓝图驱动方法相较于递归分解的优越性。

Abstract: We introduce Goedel-Architect, an agentic framework for formal theorem proving in Lean 4 centered on blueprint generation and refinement. A blueprint is a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas that builds up to the main theorem. First, Goedel-Architect generates a blueprint of formally stated definitions and lemmas, along with declared dependencies. This blueprint is optionally guided by a natural language proof. Then, a tool-equipped Lean prover component closes each open lemma node in parallel using relevant dependencies. Failed lemmas in turn drive refinement of the global blueprint. This strategy contrasts with other mainstream approaches which use recursive lemma decomposition, and can inefficiently loop on dead-end strategies. Using the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) as the backbone, Goedel-Architect attains 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% pass@1 on PutnamBench. With an optional natural-language proof seeding the initial blueprint on the harder problems, we additionally close the remaining two MiniF2F-test problems (reaching 100%), lift PutnamBench to 88.8% (597/672), and solve 4/6 on IMO 2025, 11/12 on Putnam 2025, and 3/6 on USAMO 2026. This represents state-of-the-art performance for an open-source pipeline at a price point up to 500x less than comparable open-source pipelines.

</details>


### [333] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve 是一个基于大语言模型的自进化多智能体框架，通过渐进式 MCGS、回顾性记忆和分层控制，在长周期机器学习算法发现任务中实现了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLE 智能体存在分支间信息隔离、无记忆搜索和缺乏分层控制的问题，限制了其在长周期任务中的自我进化能力。

Method: 1) 渐进式 MCGS：通过图结构参考边实现跨分支信息流动，结合熵启发的渐进调度从广泛探索过渡到聚焦利用；2) 回顾性记忆：冷启动领域知识库 + 动态全局记忆，实现任务特定经验的检索与重用；3) 自适应编码模式：将策略规划与代码生成解耦，支持稳定长周期迭代。

Result: 在 MLE-Bench 上，MLEvolve 在 12 小时预算（标准运行时间的一半）下，在平均奖牌率和有效提交率等多个维度取得 SOTA 性能；在数学算法优化任务上超越 AlphaEvolve 等专门方法，展现出强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve 通过图搜索与记忆机制的结合，有效解决了 LLM 智能体在长周期自进化任务中的关键瓶颈，验证了跨域泛化的可行性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [334] [Gradient descent at the Edge of Stability: free energy model and kinetic description of the two-layer network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05326)
*Antonin Chodron de Courcel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了梯度下降在"稳定性边缘"（Edge of Stability）动态下的行为，提出了一个连续时间有效模型来追踪平均轨迹与快速振荡的时间平均协方差，揭示了有效自由能（原始风险函数加上曲率相关的"熵"项）是监控此类不稳定动态的关键量，并针对宽两层神经网络推导了平均场极限下的动理学方程，最后通过矩阵分解和深度学习任务验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降在稳定性边缘（大学习率导致损失和锐度持续振荡）的动态行为，建立能够描述该不稳定区域中平均轨迹与快速振荡耦合演化的理论框架。

Method: 提出连续时间有效模型，追踪平均轨迹与快速振荡的时间平均协方差；引入有效自由能（原始风险+曲率相关的"熵"项）；针对宽两层神经网络推导平均场极限，得到描述权重及其波动联合分布的动理学方程，并证明该方程是Wasserstein-2梯度流。

Result: 有效自由能能够监控不稳定动态；模型可追踪振荡包络线，即使平均权重与振荡动态时间尺度相近时依然有效；在矩阵分解和CIFAR-10深度学习任务上验证了模型捕捉振荡包络线的准确性及有效自由能的预测能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的有效模型能够准确描述大学习率下梯度下降的振荡动态，有效自由能是分析稳定性边缘训练行为的关键工具，为理解现代神经网络训练中普遍存在的不稳定现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of gradient descent in the Edge of Stability regime, where the learning rate is large enough to induce persistent oscillations in the loss and the sharpness. We propose a continuous-time effective model that tracks the evolution of the average trajectory coupled with the time-averaged covariance of its fast oscillations. Our analysis reveals that the natural quantity to monitor in such unstable regimes is an effective free energy, which combines the original risk functional with a curvature-related "entropic" term. Our model allows us to track the envelope of the oscillations even in situations where its dynamics evolve on similar timescales as the averaged weights. Otherwise stated, we can track the spikes that occur during the training of some neural network architectures. For wide two-layer neural networks optimized under stable non-vanishing oscillations, we derive a mean-field limit that results in a novel kinetic equation describing the joint distribution of weights and their fluctuations. We show that this equation can be interpreted as a Wasserstein-2 gradient flow of a macroscopic free energy. Finally, we provide numerical evidence on matrix factorization and deep learning tasks (CIFAR-10) to demonstrate the model's accuracy in capturing the envelope of the oscillations and the predictive power of the effective free energy.

</details>


### [335] [Optimal experimental design for passive imaging source problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05490)
*Christian Aarset,Thorsten Hohage,Georg Stadler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对被动成像中的最优实验设计（OED）问题，提出了一种基于贝叶斯反演框架的两层级低秩近似方法，通过将A-最优设计目标分解为离线与在线阶段，有效克服了传感器数量带来的计算瓶颈，数值实验表明该方法在大规模问题中高效且优于随机传感器布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动成像中，使用相关数据导致观测维度随传感器数量二次增长，给最优设计带来计算困难；现有方法中反复求解偏微分方程（PDE）构成主要计算瓶颈。

Method: 采用贝叶斯反演框架处理被动成像源问题，针对Helmholtz方程控制的空间非相关源；提出A-最优设计目标的两层级低秩近似，将问题分解为离线阶段（低秩分解）与在线阶段（快速评估），避免额外的PDE求解。

Result: 所提算法能高效扩展至大规模问题；与随机传感器布局相比，最优设计在最小化后验不确定性方面显著更优。

Conclusion: 两层低秩近似方法有效解决了被动成像OED的计算瓶颈，为大规模传感器优化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This work focuses on optimal experimental design (OED) methods for passive imaging. We adopt a Bayesian inverse problem framework for passive imaging source problems, primarily focusing on spatially uncorrelated sources and systems governed by the Helmholtz equation. A major challenge in passive imaging is that the use of correlation data causes the observation dimension to grow quadratically with the number of sensor locations, compounding the computational difficulty of finding optimal designs. To overcome the computational bottleneck of repeated PDE solves in optimal design algorithms, we develop a two-level, low-rank approximation of the A-optimal design objective. This effectively decouples the problem into an offline and an online phase, enabling efficient evaluation of the design objective and its gradient without additional PDE solves. Our numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm efficiently scales to large problems and that the resulting optimal designs significantly outperform random sensor placements in minimizing posterior uncertainty.

</details>


### [336] [Convergence rate of the Halpern iterations with possibly distinct anchor and initial guess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05630)
*Jianing He,Qiao-Li Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了锚点与初始猜测不同的广义Halpern迭代的收敛速率，给出了预设和自适应锚定参数的紧致收敛速率估计，推广了现有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Halpern迭代的收敛速率估计仅限于锚点与初始猜测相同的特殊情况，缺乏对更一般情形（锚点与初始猜测不同）的理论分析。

Method: 针对广义Halpern迭代，分别考虑预设锚定参数和自适应锚定参数两种情形，推导出紧致的收敛速率估计。

Result: 给出了预设和自适应锚定参数下广义Halpern迭代的紧致收敛速率估计，并推广了已有的相关结果。

Conclusion: 本文将Halpern迭代的收敛速率分析从特殊情形推广到一般情形，为锚点和初始猜测不同的迭代提供了理论保证。

Abstract: All existing convergence rate estimates of Halpern iterations are established only for the case where the anchor coincides with the initial guess. This paper aims to investigate the convergence rate of general Halpern iterations, where the anchor and the initial guess may not necessarily be the same. We present tight convergence rate estimates for both predetermined and adaptive anchoring parameters. These results generalize existing related work.

</details>


### [337] [Combining diffuse and sharp interface methods in shape optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05963)
*Philip J. Herbert,Michael Hinze,Christian Kahle*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合相场法和尖锐界面法的两阶段形状优化策略：先用相场法确定优化域的拓扑和大致形状，再以尖锐界面法在Lipschitz拓扑下进行精细优化，并详细描述了从相场有限元网格到尖锐界面法初始网格的构建过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场法擅长确定形状、尺寸和拓扑结构，但难以锐化出拐角等尖锐特征；而尖锐界面法恰好能处理这些精细结构。将两者结合可优势互补。

Method: 两阶段流程：第一阶段用相场法确定优化形状；第二阶段以相场解经后处理的零水平集构建有限元网格，作为尖锐界面法的初始解，在Lipschitz拓扑下进行形状优化。两种方法均采用有限元离散。

Result: 在多个文献和应用的测试问题上验证了该方法的有效性，表明两阶段结合能够同时处理拓扑变化和尖锐几何特征。

Conclusion: 相场法与尖锐界面法的结合是一种有效的形状优化策略，能够克服各自局限，适用于需要兼顾拓扑灵活性和几何精度的形状优化问题。

Abstract: We develop a concept for the numerical treatment of shape optimization problems based on the combination of phase field and sharp interface methods. On the one hand, phase field methods are very well suited to numerically determine the shape, size and topology of a sought domain, but on the other hand they have problems to sharpen out domains where they e.g. should develop corners. However, this is the strength of a sharp-interface approach developed in our group, which provides shape updates in the Lipschitz topology. This leads to a two-stage process that first determines an optimized shape using the phase field method. The resulting domain is the starting solution for the sharp interface shape optimization method. Both methods are discretized with the finite element method. The starting mesh for the sharp method is constructed from the finite element mesh of the optimal phase field solution using its properly post processed zero-level set. We describe this construction process in detail and investigate the performance of our method on a selection of test problems from the literature and from applications.

</details>


### [338] [Uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers and associated qualification conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06067)
*Patrick Mehlitz,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象空间中约束优化问题拉格朗日乘子的唯一性与稳定性，证明严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件蕴含拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立平静性，进而保证乘子唯一性；同时表明该条件足以推导RZK约束规格，并在附加假设下与之等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究约束优化问题中拉格朗日乘子的唯一性和稳定性这一基础理论问题，尤其是孤立平静性这一集值映射单边Lipschitz性质在其中的作用机制。

Method: 从严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件出发，分析其对拉格朗日乘子映射孤立平静性的蕴含关系；通过必要条件推导和等价性证明建立理论联系，辅以反例验证假设的必要性。

Result: 证明了严格RZK条件是拉格朗日乘子映射孤立平静性的充分条件，且该条件足以推出RZK约束规格；在附加假设下，该条件与严格RZK条件等价。

Conclusion: 严格RZK条件通过保证拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立平静性，为乘子的唯一性和稳定性提供了理论保证；所得等价关系深化了对约束规格与乘子性质之间联系的理解。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers for constrained optimization problems in abstract spaces. It is well known that validity of the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition implies the so-called isolated calmness, a one-sided Lipschitz property tailored for set-valued mappings, of some Lagrange multiplier mapping associated with a perturbed version of the original optimization problem, and the latter indeed is enough to guarantee uniqueness of the Lagrange multiplier. The paper studies the isolated calmness of the Lagrange multiplier mapping in detail. Exemplary, it is shown that this condition is sufficient for the Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz constraint qualification and, in the presence of additional assumptions, even equivalent to the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition. Illustrative examples are presented to underline the necessity of postulated assumptions.

</details>


### [339] [Second order splitting dynamics for stochastic monotone inclusions with closed loop distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06280)
*Wutao Si,Hamza Ennaji,Jalal Fadili*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了希尔伯特空间中求解极大单调算子与余强制算子之和的零点问题的连续时间二阶动力学，该问题自然涵盖了具有决策依赖分布的随机优化（即表现性预测），并在均匀单调性假设下证明了轨线的强收敛性和快速渐近收敛速度，以及在强单调条件下建立了全局指数收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究希尔伯特空间中极大单调算子与余强制算子之和的零点问题，其自然涵盖了表现性预测（performative prediction）中的决策依赖分布随机优化问题，旨在为此类问题设计具有良好收敛性质的连续时间动力学方法。

Method: 提出由分布评估的前向-后向分裂算子驱动的连续时间二阶动力学系统，采用消失粘性阻尼系数；在强单调情形下使用常数Polyak型阻尼系数，分析轨线的强收敛性与收敛速率。

Result: 在一般均匀单调性假设下建立了平衡点的存在唯一性，证明了轨线强收敛至平衡点且速度具有快速渐近收敛率；在强单调算子情形下获得了全局指数收敛速率。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶动力学系统能够有效求解极大单调算子与余强制算子之和的零点问题，为表现性预测等决策依赖分布优化问题提供了理论保证和高效的连续时间求解框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the problem of finding a zero of the sum of a maximal monotone operator $A$ and a cocoercive operator $\Bm$ in a Hilbert space. This formulation naturally captures stochastic optimization problems with decision-dependent distributions, often referred to as performative prediction. We propose and analyze continuous-time second-order dynamics governed by a distributionally evaluated forward-backward splitting operator. We establish the existence and uniqueness of the equilibrium point under a general uniform monotonicity assumption. In this setting, employing a vanishing viscous damping coefficient, we prove the strong convergence of the trajectories to the equilibrium, accompanied by fast asymptotic convergence rates for the velocities. Furthermore, when the regularizing operator is strongly monotone, we consider a constant Polyak-type damping coefficient and we establish global exponential convergence rates for the dynamical system.

</details>
